# Fire Sale
Aliases: Celestial Goldor Skin, Celestial Maxor Skin, Celestial Necron Skin, Celestial Storm Skin, Fire Sales, Ornamental Rune, Ornamental Rune III
Categories: Community Center, Events, Historical content
[Fire Sale]
Fire Sales are events hosted by [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]], where players can purchase exclusive cosmetic items for [[SkyBlock Gems]]. Fire Sale stock is limited and never returns once a sale has ended.

# Glossary
Categories: Community Guides
- This article is a guide.
- This article has been assembled by members of the community as a helpful resource or [[Tutorial]], instructing players on [[SkyBlock]], and thus is not official.
- If you see missing, incorrect or out of date information, please help by contributing to the page.
The following is a list of terms and abbreviations commonly used within the SkyBlock community.
# represents any number.
## #
- #₀/#₁ (Dungeons) - Number of secrets out of total secrets in the current room. For example, 7/8 means 7 secrets found of 8 total
- #₁m #₂a #₃h #₄b #₅t (Dungeons) - The desired class composition for a Dungeon party. Any of the parts may be omitted. For example, 3m 1a 1h represents 3 Mages, 1 Archer and 1 Healer.
- #c (Dungeons) - Number of Crypts destroyed in the run. For example, 4c means 4 crypts
- OFA/1FA - [[One For All]]
- XP run - A technique where the Dungeon party plays until 1 minute has elapsed, then dies for XP.
- 1 tap - Killing a mob in a single hit
- 1-tick - Mining a block in a single Minecraft tick (or 0.05 second). See also instamining.
- #-ticking - Mining a block in # Minecraft ticks. Mostly used within the [[Mining]] community, most notably in the context of [[Gemstone]] Mining.
- 3-5 business days - 1471 days, the time it took for the [[Wizard]] to repair his portal and open [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]]. Used as a joke in the community when something is stated to come "soon".
- 30 (Player) - thirtyvirus
- 50m Midas - [[Midas' Sword]] with 50M [[Coins]] paid
- 80% run - A technique where the Dungeon party rushes until the dungeon is 80% complete, then dies for XP.
## A
- A (Dungeons) - [[Archer]]
- AA - [[Angry Archaeologist]]
- Acc - [[Accessories]]
- AD - [[Ability Damage]]
- After (Cata F6) - see Golems after
- AH - [[Auction House]]
- AH flipping - Buying items off [[Auction House]] for low prices and reselling them for higher prices.
- Alch - [[Alchemy]]
- AOTD - [[Aspect of the Dragons]]
- AOTDd - Hit with the [[Aspect of the Dragons]] ability, often with negative results
- AOTE - [[Aspect of the End]]
- AOTJ - [[Aspect of the Jerry]]
- AOTS - [[Halberd of the Shredded]] (formerly Axe of the Shredded)
- AOTV - [[Aspect of the Void]]
- Arch - [[Archer]]
- Archer mid (Cata F7) - Instructions for the Archer to go to the middle platform immediately after starting phase 4 of [[Necron]]
- Arrow (Cata F7) - The arrow device in the 3rd part of the 3rd phase of [[Necron]]
- AS - [[Attack Speed]]
## B
- B (Dungeons) - [[Berserk]]
- BAS - [[Attack Speed]]
- Bers - [[Berserk]]
- Beheaded - [[Beheaded Horror]]
- Bone - [[Bonemerang]]
- Box (Dungeons) - Boulder Puzzle
- Blaze (Dungeons) - Higher or Lower Puzzle
- Blue (item) - [[Rarity|RARE]]
- BP - [[Backpack]]
- BR (Dungeons) - Blood Rush, when 1 player rushes to the Blood Room as quickly as possible
- BW - [[Blue Whale Pet]]
- BZ - [[Bazaar]]
## C
- Cata - [[Catacombs]]
- CC - [[Crit Chance]]
- CD - [[Crit Damage]]
- Cheese - A strategy to complete a task in an unintended and faster way.
- Chest (Armor) - Chestplate
- Clean - An unmodified item
- Collat - Collateral
- Collateral - A guarantee in the form of money or items, given when trusting another player with an item for them to borrow or upgrade
- Comm - [[Commissions|Commission]]
- Cookie - [[Booster Cookie]]
- CP - Chestplate
## D
- DA - [[Dark Auction]]
- DC (Cata F7) - see Downcrush
- DC (General) - Disconnect (from the server)
- Def - [[Defense]]
- DH (Enchantment) - [[Gravity|Dragon Hunter]]
- DH (Location) - [[Dungeon Hub]]
- Dia - Diamond
- Dmg - [[Damage]]
- DOT - Damage over Time
- Downcrush (Cata F7) - Crushing Necron with a downwards movement of the pillar.
- DP - see Dragon party
- DPH - Damage per hit
- DPS - Damage per second
- Drag - [[Ender Dragon]]
- Dragon party - A party of typically 2-4 people, each with [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]], who team up to spawn a Dragon
- Dream (Player) - A Minecraft YouTuber. He received the 8th most votes in the Resistance Elections despite not being a SkyBlock player.
- Drop blocks - A request for another player to drop [[Enchanted Diamond Block|Enchanted Diamond Blocks]] to pick up and sell to the Trade menu. Used when one cannot afford to unlock a [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] with a valuable reward inside. Obsolete since 2021 as players cannot perceive items dropped by other players anymore.
- Drop recomb - A request for another player to drop [[Recombobulator 3000]] to pick up and sell to the Bazaar. Used when one cannot afford to unlock a [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] with a valuable reward inside. Obsolete since 2021 as players cannot perceive items dropped by other players anymore.
- DSM - Danker's SkyBlock Mod
- Dung - [[Dungeons]]
- During (Cata F6) - see Golems during
- Dupe - Item duplication exploit
## E
- EBow - [[Explosive Bow]]
- EC - Ender Chest
- EChest - Ender Chest
- Edrag - [[Ender Dragon Pet]]
- Eff - [[Efficiency]]
- EHP - [[Effective Health]]
- Em blade - [[Emerald Blade]]
- Eme blade - [[Emerald Blade]]
- Ench (Item) - Enchanted
- Ench (Skill) - [[Enchanting]]
- Ess - [[Essence]]
- Exotic - An armor piece with a rare color. For example, a [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Chestplate]] with hex color #000000
## F
- F# (Dungeons) - Floor #. For example, F7 is Floor 7
- F0 (Dungeons) - Entrance floor
- FA - [[Frozen Adventurer]]
- FB - [[Frozen Blaze Armor]]
- FD - [[Final Destination Armor]]
- Fer - [[Ferocity]]
- Fero - [[Ferocity]]
- FHPB - [[Fuming Potato Book]]
- FoT - [[Flower of Truth]]
- FPB - [[Fuming Potato Book]]
- Frag - Fragment
- Fragged - Upgraded with Fragments
- Frag bot - A bot which automatically accepts party invites and leaves after a short time. They are used to enter Dungeons solo.
- Frag run - A technique where a player enters a Dungeon (often solo), kills mobs which drop Fragments and leaves.
- FS - [[Frozen Scythe]]
- FSR - FurfSky Reborn texture pack
## G
- G#P# (where # is a number) - [[Growth]] and [[Protection]] of tiers #
- GDrag/Greg - [[Golden Dragon Pet]]
- GK - [[Giant Killer]]
- Glitched chunk - A glitch in Dungeons where entities in a certain chunk will freeze
- Godpot - [[God Potion]]
- Gogs (item) - Goggles ([[Dark Goggles]], [[Shadow Goggles]], [[Wither Goggles]])
- Going (Dungeons) - Entering the Dungeon or the boss room
- Golem (End) - [[End Stone Protector]]
- Golem run - A technique where a player enters [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]], rushes to the boss, kills the Golems and leaves
- Golems after (Cata F6) - Instruction to kill the Woke Golems after the Terracotta phase in [[Sadan]]
- Golems during (Cata F6) - Instruction to kill the Woke Golems during the Terracotta phase in [[Sadan]]
- Green (Cata F7) - Green pillar
- Green (Item) - [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]
- Griffin event - [[Mythological Ritual]]
- GS - [[Giant's Sword]]
## H
- Hael9 - A hacked client for SkyBlock. Also the name of its creator.
- Handle - [[Necron's Handle]]
- HotM - [[Heart of the Mountain]]
- HotF - [[Heart of the Forest]]
- HPB - [[Hot Potato Book]]
- Hyp - [[Hyperion]]
- Hype - [[Hyperion]]
- Hypermaxed - see Overmaxed
- Hyp m - [[Mage]] with [[Hyperion]]
- H pad (Cata F7) - Instructions for the [[Healer]] to stand on the pad to crush [[Necron]] in phase 2
## I
- Inquis - [[Minos Inquisitor]]
- Instamine, Instamining - To fully mine and break a block in a single click. See also 1-tick.
- Inv (party) - Party invite
## J
- JCG - [[Jerry-chine Gun]]
- Juju - [[Juju Shortbow]]
- Juju non - A user who uses the [[Juju Shortbow]] to essentially skip progression. They generally have very low stats and [[Accessory Power]], resulting in very low survivability.
## K
- KB - Knockback
- Key (Dungeons) - Wither Key or Blood Key
- Kismet - [[Kismet Feather]]
## L
- L - Short for "loss" or "loser"; an insult
- LA - [[Lost Adventurer]]
- Leap - [[Spirit Leap]] (to someone)
- Left (Cata F7) - The crystal on the left side in phase 1 of [[Necron]]
- Leg - [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] (item)
- Lights (Cata F7) - Lights device in part 2 of phase 3 of [[Necron]]
- Livid (weapon) - [[Livid Dagger]]
## M
- M (Dungeons) - [[Mage]]
- M# (Dungeons) - Master Floor #
- Maxed (item) - Upgraded with basic upgrades such as 5 stars and [[Sharpness|Sharpness V]]
- MF - [[Magic Find]]
- Midas Spoon - [[Midas Staff]]
## N
- NEU - NotEnoughUpdates mod
- NL - Necromancer Lord
- No PF - No prefire, do not prefire
- Non
1. Someone with default rank on Hypixel network, derogatory
2. A player who is bad, in terms of gear or skill, and usually has low [[Accessory Power]]. See Juju non.
- NPF - No prefire, do not prefire
- NW (Farming) - [[Nether Wart]]
- NW (Stat) - Net worth
- ND (Dungeons) - No Dupes(duplicate roles)
## O
- O4A (Enchantment) - [[One For All]]
- OFA (Enchantment) - [[One For All]]
- OL - [[Overload]]
- One cycle (Cata F7) - Dealing enough damage to [[Necron]] on the first pillar in phase 2 to immediately continue to phase 3.
- One pillar (Cata F7) - see One cycle
- Overmaxed - Upgraded with expensive upgrades
- Overpay - Paying more than what an item is worth (usually in [[Dark Auction]])
## P
- P# (Dungeons) - Phase # (of the boss)
- P# (Bows) - [[Power]] #
- P2W - The mechanic of buying in-game items with real money.
- PC#K - [[Personal Compactor 4000|Personal Compactor #000]]
- PF (Cata F7) - Prefire
- Ping Gliding - A technique developed primarily in [[Mining]] communities, where a player with high ping will assume they are a "step ahead" of the servers and move on to break another block before the server has shown them the visual confirmation that the block they were on was broken.
- Pink (item) - [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]
- PL - [[Pet Luck]]
- Prefire (Cata F7) - Shooting the Wither Skeletons on the ground when midair going from phase 1 to phase 2 of [[Necron]].
- Purple (Cata F7) - Purple pillar
- Purple (item) - [[Rarity|EPIC]]
- PY (Cata F7) - Where the [[Tank]] solo crushes [[Storm]] at purple pad and then the yellow pad, effectively halving the [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Storm Phase's]] time.
## Q
- Q - Queue
## R
- R (Dungeons) - Ready
- RC3K - [[Recombobulator 3000]]
- Recomb - [[Recombobulator 3000]]
- Recombed - Item with [[Recombobulator 3000]] applied
- Reparty - Where the party leader, often automatically, disbands the party and reinvites all its members.
- Rev (Slayer) - [[Revenant Horror]] or pertaining to [[Zombie Slayer]]
- Right (Cata F7) - The crystal on the right side in phase 1 of [[Necron]]
- RNG - Random number generator; Randomness; referring to rare drops or events
- RoC - [[Rod of Champions]]
- RoL - [[Rod of Legends]]
- Rose run - see Golem run
- RotS - [[Rod of the Sea]]
- RP - Reparty
- RQ (Dungeons) - Requeue (in party finder)
- RS - Restart (the Dungeon run)
## S
- S# (Cata F7) - Refers to the different sections of [[Goldor]]'s phase, ex: S2 would refer to the second section of [[Terminal|Terminals]]
- S1 (General) - Someone
- SA - [[Shadow Assassin]]
- SB - SkyBlock
- SBA - SkyBlock Addons mod
- SBE - SkyBlock Extras mod
- SC (Fishing) - Sea Creature
- SC3K - [[Super Compactor 3000]]
- SCC - [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- SE - [[Soul Eater]]
- SF - [[Shadow Fury]]
- SH - SkyHanni mod
- Shard (Slayer) - [[Shredded Sinew]]
- Sharp - [[Sharpness]]
- Shelm - [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]]
- Shelmet - [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]]
- Spoon - [[Midas Staff]]
- Squid (Player) - im_a_squid_kid
- Squid fishing - Fishing in [[The Park]] with rain for Squids
- SS (Cata F7/M7) - Simon Says device in S1 of [[Terminal|Terminals]] phase
- SS (General) - Screenshot
- Str - [[Strength]]
- Sub # (Dungeons/Kuudra) - Requirement to complete the run within # minutes. For example, sub 10 means completion within 10 minutes.
- Sup (Armor) - [[Superior Dragon Armor]]
- Sup (End) - Superior Dragon
- Sven - [[Sven Packmaster]] or pertaining to [[Wolf Slayer]]
- swavy (Player) - see 56ms
## T
- T (Dungeons) - [[Tank]]
- T# (General) - Tier #
- T6 - An enchant that is classed as Tier 6, such as [[Sharpness|Sharpness VI]] and [[Drain|Drain IV]]
- T7 - An enchant that is classed as Tier 7, such as [[Sharpness|Sharpness VII]] and [[First Strike|First Strike V]]
- Talis - [[Accessories|Accessory]]
- Tara - [[Tarantula Broodfather]] or pertaining to [[Spider Slayer]]
- TB (Guild) - Trouble Brewing
- TB (item) - [[Tier Boost]]
- Term (Cata F7) - [[Terminal]]
- Term (Weapon) - [[Terminator]]
- Ti (Mining) - [[Titanium]]
- Tita (Mining) - [[Titanium]]
- Throw - Causing a Dungeon run to fail or being the reason a certain score, usually S+, is not achieved
- Trap (Dungeons) - Danger room
## U
- UC (Cata F7) - Upcrush
- Upc (Cata F7) - Upcrush
- Upcrush (Cata F7) - A technique where players use [[Jerry-chine Gun]] or other methods to boost themselves upwards, causing Necron to move upwards into a pillar when it is being raised. This counts as a crush.
## V
- Valk - [[Valkyrie]]
## W
- Water (Dungeons) - Water Board puzzle
- WC - Wrong chat/Wrong channel
- Wipe - Glitch which causes items to be deleted
- White (item) - [[Rarity|COMMON]]
## Y
- Yeti (Pet) - [[Baby Yeti Pet]]
## Z
- ZK - Zombie Knight
- ZS - Zombie Soldier

# Tutorial
Aliases: Community Tutorials, Dungeons Guide, Guide, Guides, Help, Progression, Tutorials
Categories: Community Guides
- This article is a guide.
- This article has been assembled by members of the community as a helpful resource or Tutorial, instructing players on [[SkyBlock]], and thus is not official.
- If you see missing, incorrect or out of date information, please help by contributing to the page.
Community tutorials are written by players for other players.
Welcome to Community Tutorials, a place to write tutorials to guide other players to play [[SkyBlock]]! You can read, write and share your ideas with your Tutorials.
- Any amount of advice is allowed, your creativity is the only limit!
- Please help us to update some outdated tutorials.
- If you'd like examples of well-built articles as a basis for your tutorials, we recommend checking out Fishing Guide or the Mage Guide.
- Browse the list in later sections for useful tutorials.
  See Category:Tutorials or Tutorials for all tutorials.
## Rules for Tutorials
- The tutorial must be created with the Tutorial: namespace.
- The tutorial must have {{Guide}} at the top of the page.
- The tutorial must have the "Tutorials" category.
- The tutorial title must not contain the following character: @

# Community Resources
Aliases: Resources
Categories: Community Resource
This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
The Allay was chosen to be the mascot for the Community Resources project.
Community Resources are resources assembled by the players of SkyBlock to help other players of the community.
## Guidelines
These guidelines outline where information belongs. For example, guilds should belong under guilds. Unlike other pages on the wiki, the rules on advertising are not as strict here since you can advertise your guild here.
### Guilds
All Guild pages should belong under the [[Community Resources:Guilds]]. You may create your own guild page in any format under the guilds page.
## Resources
### Websites
  See: [[Community Resources:Useful External Resources]]
Useful SkyBlock-related websites.
### Mods
  See: [[Community Resources:Mods]]
These mods are popular in Hypixel SkyBlock and are suggested to install.
### Resource Packs
  See: [[Community Resources:Resource Packs]]
Popular resource packs to make SkyBlock look your own way.
### Discord Servers and Integrations
All Discord related links:
- [[Community Resources:Discords]]
- [[Community Resources:Discord Bots]]
These are trusted and popular in Hypixel SkyBlock and suggested to use.
### Guilds
  See: [[Community Resources:Guilds]]
These are Hypixel SkyBlock-centric guilds.

# Community Resources:Discord Bots
Aliases: Community Resources/Discord Bots
Categories: Community Resource, Bots
This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
Discord is an instant messaging and voice chat app/site mainly used by gamers that offers a lot of features not to be found on most messaging apps. It displays the game you're playing, allows for the creation of servers which can have multiple text and voice channels dedicated to different topics and more. Bots are similar to normal accounts, except they are controlled by code.
## Discord Bots List
Guidelines:
- This is a non-exhaustive list of Discord bots that aid gameplay.
- If you have valid reasons to believe that a Discord bot should not be added, please discuss on the talk page.
Main Content:
|Bot name|Link|Uses|Default prefix|
|-|-|-|-|
|CommunityBot|https://discord.com/application-directory/854722092037701643; https://top.gg/bot/854722092037701643|Offers an in-depth networth calculation, networth leaderboard, displays various player stats, let's you check the 10 lowest BINs, extensive bazaar price checker, recommends the cheapest minions to upgrade and shows which accessories you are missing! More features coming in frequent updates.|Dot .|
|Hypixel Skybot|https://top.gg/bot/630106665387032576|Searches player stats, auctions of a player, price of item in the bazaar, price of an item in lowest price BIN, timers for everything, recent updates, best item to flip, memes, and more!|Bang !|
|Jerry the Price Checker|https://top.gg/bot/614661627513536526|Checks the price of items in auction and bazaar, can also be used to detect scammers|Dollar Sign $|
|Kuudra Bot|https://discord.com/application-directory/1040668238318669824; https://top.gg/de/bot/1040668238318669824|A bot focused on the Kuudra boss. Searches for all items with specific attributes with additional search settings, calculates items and money needed for specific upgrades in a form you can specify your armor upgrade including a spend limit of each item or coins.|Slash /|
|Project Alpha|https://top.gg/bot/656739867404795934|Calculates networth, lists missing talismans, optimizes stats, views a player's profile, and views item prices|alpha|
|Skyblock Simplified|https://hypixel.net/threads/skyblock-simplified-home-of-official-skyblock-simplified-reforge-optimizer-bot.3493132/|Optimizes reforges, lists missing talismans from cheapest to most expensive, lets you see other people's stats, and lets you see entire guild stats and more|sbs|
|SkyHelper|https://discord.com/application-directory/710143953533403226; https://top.gg/bot/710143953533403226|A useful bot that lets you check skills, prices, and overall further progress in the game of Hypixel Skyblock.|Slash /|
|Statisfy (aka. Hystats)|https://statsify.net/; https://discord.com/application-directory/718827787422793820|A general Hypixel bot, can display Hypixel stats and stats for other games|s!|

# Community Resources:Discords
Aliases: Community Resources/Discords, Discords
Categories: Community Resource
This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
The following is a list of Hypixel SkyBlock Discord servers. Players can join any of them.
## Discord Servers List
Guidelines:
- This list is a non-exhaustive list of Discord servers that aids gameplay.
- If you have valid reasons to believe that a Discord server should not be added, please discuss on the talk page.
Main Content:
|Name|Invite|Features|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|Bingo Brewers|https://discord.gg/bingobrewers|Bingo Splashes|Provides potion splashes for Bingo players.|
|Bingo Splash Community|https://discord.gg/bingo|Bingo Splashes|Provides potion splashes for Bingo players.|
|Casters|https://discord.gg/XGc26ueGFs|Fishing Guides and Discussion|Provides guides and a space for discussion for fishers.|
|Cowshed|https://discord.gg/f6N7RmA|SkyBlock News|Announces [[SkyBlock]] News, Version Changes ([[Changelog]]), Content Updates ([[Changelog]]), leaks and more.|
|Dungeon Secret Guides|https://discord.gg/QXtNVMt|Guides to Dungeons|Provides the locations of the secrets in Dungeons, sorted by room size and secret count.|
|Dungeon Hub|https://discord.gg/dungeons|Dungeon Carries and Looking for Group|Provides Dungeon Carries and can be used to find Dungeon parties.|
|Elite SkyBlock Farmers|https://discord.gg/farms|Farming weights, farming help|A server for serious farmers who wish to upgrade and share their skills in farming.|
|Farming Council (inactive)|https://discord.gg/farming-council-skyblock-guides-more-1020742260683448450|Farming guides|Provides tutorials, guides and help for farming|
|Ironman Sweats|https://discord.gg/ims|Ironman Gamemode|Server for players of the ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) gamemode on SkyBlock.|
|Kuudra Gang|https://discord.gg/kuudra|Guides, Carries, and other features|A friendly environment completely focused on Kuudra. Offers everything what new and more advanced Kuudra Gamers need: Updates about Kuudra, advice channel for any questions, fast completion carry services and active party finding|
|Official Hunters Discord|https://discord.gg/tvb55qGnFU|Hunts & assistance with obtaining the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]]|A server that focuses on SkyBlock-related hunts, and offers free services to help with obtaining the [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]], and [[Kuudra Follower Relic]].|
|SkyBlock Simplified|https://discord.gg/sbs|Trading & Services, Bot|General purpose SkyBlock server that facilitates player-to-player trading and services with a reputation system. Also has a bot which is known for its talisman reforge optimizer.|
|Skyblock Maniacs|https://discord.gg/sbm|Dungeon Carries, Services|Has a set group of service providers with a reputation system rather than player-to-player. Provides mainly Carries, though has other services.|
|SkyBlockZ|https://discord.gg/skyblock|Trading & Services|General purpose SkyBlock server that facilitates player-to-player trading and services with a reputation system. Also known for its extensive scammer list and bot to search the record of a player.|
|Stranded Sweats|https://discord.gg/qshF9vD4x3|Stranded Gamemode|Server for players of the ☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) gamemode on SkyBlock.|
|[GER] Hypixel's Skyblock Community (DrachenKult)|https://discord.gg/drachenkult|Trading & Services Handel & Dienstleistungen|Largest German Hypixel Skyblock community. Größte deutsche Hypixel Skyblock-Community.|

# Community Resources:Mods
Aliases: Community Resources/Mods, Mods
Categories: Community Resource
This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
Minecraft modifications are a large part of the Minecraft experience for many players, with modifications ranging from small performance and visual improvements right through to gameplay altering ones.
## Summary
As Minecraft modifications provide such a wide range of functionality, not all modifications are permitted to be used on Hypixel. The modifications below are use at your own risk. They are highly trusted modifications though and will likely not result in a ban, however user discretion is still advised.
It's advised to download mods from the official discords or websites of mod developers. Links from other sources might be a token logger, otherwise known as a rat. These mods can steal not only your Hypixel SkyBlock items, but your Minecraft account in general.
As of March 26, 2021, Hypixel Admin Fr0z3n released a thread clarifying which type of mods may result in a ban. This was done because some features in mods were flagging Watchdog, causing false bans. Mods that would modify packets and automate clicks are some examples.
Mods that alter the following are deemed illegal:
- Displaying invisible entities (i.e. Ghosts, Fels).
- Changing player movements.
- Cancelling vanilla actions like clicking on blocks or interacting with a Player, Monster, or [[NPC]].
  - This does not impact the cancelling of actions such as "not dropping/salvaging certain items".
- Automatically interacting with interface elements, such as inventories or menus. (i.e. automatically solving terminals/experimentation tables).
## Mods
Guidelines:
- This list is a non-exhaustive list of mods that aids gameplay.
- If you have valid reasons to believe that a mod should not be added, please discuss on the talk page.
Main Content:
### Base Mods
|Modification|Uses|
|-|-|
|Fabric Loader|Mandatory for running all mods below.|
|Fabric API|Mandatory for running all mods below.|
|Fabric Language Kotlin|Mandatory for running many of the mods below.|
### General SkyBlock Mods
|Modification|Uses|
|-|-|
|SkyHanni|General features to make your SkyBlock experience less frustrating.|
|Skyblocker|(same as above)|
|SkyOcean|(same as above)|
|Firmament|(same as above)|
|Skysoft|(same as above)|
|SkyBlock Profile Viewer|Adds /pv command to look up any SkyBlock player profile.|
### Other SkyBlock Mods
Modification: Uses
Odin: Adds features tailored for Dungeons and Kuudra.
Scatha-Pro: Adds achievements and other Scatha mining related features, to make it easier and more fun.
SkyblockAddons Unofficial: Fork of 1.8.9 SkyblockAddons mod. Adds miscellaneous features.
SkyBlock Item List: Adds an items menu with a search bar and recipe viewer. Alternative to REI + Firmament/Skyblocker.
Custom Scoreboard: Adds an ability to fully customize scoreboard. Replacement of the SkyHanni feature.
Skyblock Overhaul (SBO): Adds features related to the Mythological Ritual event.
### Optimization Mods
Modification: Uses
Sodium: Sodium is a powerful optimization mod for the Minecraft client, which greatly improves frame rates and micro-stutter, while fixing many graphical issues in Minecraft.
Sodium Extra: Sodium Extra aims to bring most of OptiFine's eye-candy options to Sodium; in addition to providing some features such as visual bug fixes and other performance options that are not yet available on Sodium.
Lithium: Lithium is a modern, general-purpose optimization mod for Minecraft which works to improve a number of systems (game physics, mob AI, block ticking, etc) with the goal of not changing any vanilla mechanics.
FerriteCore: This mod reduces the memory usage of Minecraft in a few different ways.
Entity Culling: This mod introduces asynchronous path-tracing to efficiently determine what's actually visible to the player. By leveraging multiple CPU cores, it calculates line-of-sight visibility in real time and eliminates unnecessary draw calls for hidden block entities and entities.
More Culling: A mod that changes how multiple types of culling are handled in order to improve performance
BadOptimizations: A collection of mostly micro optimizations that add up to something bigger!
ImmediatelyFast: ImmediatelyFast is an open source Minecraft mod which improves the immediate mode rendering performance of the client.
Particle Core: Particle Core focuses on one thing: Optimizing particles. Vanilla Minecraft is quite inefficient with particles in a variety of ways. Particle Core aims to improve this.
Ixeris: Ixeris is a mod that optimizes event polling to improve client performance.
ModernFix-mVUS: ModernFix is an all-in-one mod that improves performance, reduces memory usage, and fixes many bugs in modern Minecraft versions (most versions including and above 1.16 are supported) without majorly compromising the game experience.
ScalableLux: A Fabric mod that improves the performance of light updates in Minecraft.
Concurrent Chunk Management Engine: A Fabric mod designed to improve the chunk performance of Minecraft.
Optimized Block Entities: OBE is a client side mod that significantly improves the rendering speed of block entities.
Async Logger: Makes logging operations asynchronous, which improves performance.
Gnetum: Improves performance by distributing HUD updates over multiple frames.
### Non-SkyBlock QoL Mods
Modification: Uses
Tooltip Scroll: Adds ability to scroll any tooltip, useful when looking on items with a lot of modifiers.
Mod Menu: Adds a menu where you can easily change settings of your mods.
Sound Controller: Adds ability to change volume or disable any sound in the game.
Roughly Enough Items (REI): Adds a vanilla items menu with a search bar. Some mods, like Skyblocker or Firmament, also add SkyBlock items.
Gamma Utils: This is a fully configurable and client side gamma utility mod, making it much easier to see in the dark.
Compacting: Compacts repeated chat messages, just like Patcher did on 1.8.9.
kennytvs-epic-force-close-loading-screen-mod-for-fabric: Instantly closes the loading terrain screen on world changing and drastically reduces the resource pack loading screen duration.
More Chat History: Increases the maximum length of chat history from 100 to 16384.
### Resource Pack QoL Mods
Modification: Uses
Catharsis: Adds support for custom SkyBlock resource packs.
ResourcePackCached: Caches server resource packs (official SkyBlock pack in this case), so players don't have to wait every time they login to the server.
quick pack: Speeds up resource pack loading times.
Recursive Resources: Adds better resource pack menu.
Linked from: [[Community Resources]]

# Community Resources:Resource Packs
Aliases: Community Resources/Resource Packs, Resource Pack, Resource Packs, Resource pack, Resource packs, Texture Packs, Texture packs
Categories: Community Resource
This article is a community resource. This page has been provided as a helpful resource for Hypixel SkyBlock players.
Resource packs are bundles of assets that change the look, feel, and even sound of Minecraft. Through their use you are able to give life to custom SkyBlock gear that would otherwise look the same as Vanilla equipment. Some resource packs have been specially made for Hypixel SkyBlock and can be extremely useful as they help to differentiate between similar items. This is particularly useful for helping to prevent scams.
This page is only for Hypixel SkyBlock specific resource/texture packs, not general Vanilla ones. Please feel free to add your own pack here!
## List
The list is not in any particular order, though more popular or widespread packs are moved to the top.
|Pack|Authors|Mods Required|Information|
|-|-|-|-|
|Hypixel SkyBlock (unofficial mirror)|Hypixel|None, automatically applied when playing SkyBlock|Unofficial mirror which allows browsing all raw Hypixel SkyBlock default Resource Pack files. Can be used to create your own variations, or to better understand how the default SkyBlock textures work and changed over time.|
|FurSky Reborn|The Reborn Team|Catharsis|Hypixel Skyblock's most popular texture pack, texturing almost every item in the game and made by a team of talented volunteers.|
|Hypixel +|ic22487|None, Catharsis for player heads support|Hypixel+ is a resource pack for the Hypixel Server that textures all of SkyBlock, Bedwars, and more, with a vanilla style built to fit in with default textures new or old!|
|PacksHQ|Packs HQ|Catharsis|PacksHQ is one of Hypixel Skyblocks oldest custom texture packs! Keeping a very vanilla feel for most items, and making them more recognizable.|
|SkyBlock Dark UI|jani270|None, Firmament recommended|Textures all GUI's including GUI's from mods in a dark color.|
|Skyblock Dark Mode|Rubyrak|Firmament, Respackopts for individual toggles|Turns some locations into darker version to reduce eyestrain.|
|Sophie's Hypixel Skyblock Enchantment Books|Bentcheesee|Firmament, Respackopts for individual toggles|Adds unique texture to each enchanted book.|
|Shaded Font|philliamm|None|Add a shading effect to the vanilla Minecraft font.|
|ALAND's Hypixel SkyBlock Enchanted Books|ALAND|Firmament|Adds unique texture to each enchanted book.|
|SkyblockAddition|AlphaGenesis_|Firmament|Lightweight x32 Resource Pack|
|Arkudii's Detailed Hypixel Skyblock|Arkudii|Firmament, Respackopts for individual toggles|This Resourcepack adds more details and features to improve your gameplay in Hypixel Skyblock!|
|night's Hypixel SkyBlock Enchanted Books|nightlibra|Firmament or Catharsis|Adds unique texture to each enchanted book.|
|PHQ Garden Flowers|Packs HQ|Catharsis|Replaces Moonflower texture.|
Linked from: [[Community Resources]]

# Accessories/September 27 2021 Stacking Fixes
Aliases: Accessory/September 27 2021 Stacking Fixes
Categories: Confirmations needed
Before an update on September 27, 2021, the buffs and/or stats of certain Accessory lines would stack between tiers. Currently, only the abilities of Personal Compacter line and Personal Deleter line still work together.
All of the stacking Accessories are listed below:
- [[Master Skull]] line - Different tiers' reforges stack
- [[Titanium Talisman]] line - Different tiers' [[Mining Speed]] and reforges stack
- [[Ender Artifact]] and [[Ender Relic]] - Both tiers' abilities stack
- [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]] line - All tiers' abilities work together
- [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] line - All tiers' abilities work together, different tiers' reforges stack
- [[Beastmaster Crest]] - All rarities' Pet XP bonuses stack
- [[Razor-sharp Shark Tooth Necklace|Shark Tooth Necklace]] line - All tiers' abilities stack[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
Linked from: [[Accessories]], [[Accessories/History]]

# Autopet
Aliases: Auto Pet, Auto pet, Pet Rule, Pet Rules, Pet rule, Pet rules, Pets Auto-Equipper
Categories: Confirmations needed, Bits Shop, Mechanics, Community Center, Pets
Autopet is a mechanic that can be used to automatically equip [[Pets]] when undergoing certain actions with certain exceptions.
## Mechanics
Rules can be applied to pets that specify when they are equipped. A rule can equip a pet when a certain event occurs, with up to 5 exceptions. Only 2 may activate at the same time.
It requires at least one [[Autopet Rules 2-Pack]] to unlock, each costing 21,000 [[Bits]]. A maximum of 28 Autopet rules can be owned.
### Rules
- On login
- On gain [Skill] XP
- On enter [Island]
- On enter combat
  - This rule triggers upon dealing damage to any enemy, or taking any damage, even if that damage was not dealt by an enemy.
- On gain [Collection]
- On kill a [Mob]
  - Only vanilla mobs, as well as "Mythological Mob" and "Sea Creature" can be selected. This rule is triggered upon killing any mob with the matching vanilla form. For example, if set to Iron Golem, this rule would trigger upon killing [[Automaton]], [[Gaia Construct]], [[Sadan|Woke Golem]], or any other mob which takes the form of an Iron Golem.
  - Mobs which are player models such as [[Crypt Dreadlord]] cannot trigger this rule.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- On select dungeon [class]
  - This rule triggers upon changing to the selected class, or entering a Dungeon run with the class selected.
- On [event] start
- On [boss] spawn
- On starting a slayer quest
- On clicking the experimentation table
- On going AFK
- On digging a griffin burrow
- On opening a winter gift
- On throw fishing hook
- When you enter Catacombs Floor [floor]
### Exceptions
- Except if in [island]
- Except if NOT in [island]
  - If you have multiple Except if NOT in [island] on one rule then rather than being an OR operation they work as an AND operation. This means that multiple Except if Not in [island] cancel each other out on a single pet rule.
- Except if already has [pet] equipped
- Except if during [event]
- Except if has active slayer quest
- Except if it is [day]
- Except if you have a slayer quest with a spawned boss
## Trivia
- Initially, it was possible to set 2 pets to equip with 2 different rules at the same time, effectively gaining the benefits of both pets. For example, setting a rule to equip a [[Rabbit Pet]] when gaining Farming XP, but another to equip an [[Elephant Pet]] when increasing sugar cane collection. This way, both the Farming XP boost of the [[Rabbit Pet|Rabbit]] and the crop increase of the [[Elephant Pet|Elephant]] would take effect at the same time while farming sugar cane. This was known as pet stacking. This has since been patched, as auto-swapping pets now has a cooldown of 2 ticks (0.1 seconds).
- If multiple rules have the same trigger and both are not cancelled by an exception, it will trigger the first rule then the one after from left to right, top to bottom.
“The autopet is meant as a convenience when switching activities across SkyBlock, not as a farm boosting method.”
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Feb 3, 2021|Sneak peek at Autopet from Minikloon.|
|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Added Autopet.|
|Feb 19, 2021|Added 2 tick cooldown, patching pet stacking.|
|Feb 19, 2021|Added "On throw fishing hook" autopet rule.|
|Feb 19, 2021|Added "Except if NOT in [island]" rule exception.|
|Sep 23, 2021|Added "slayer quest with a spawned boss" exception and "enter Catacombs Floor [floor]" rule.|
|Feb 3, 2023|Changed max amount of Pet Rules to 28.|
|Aug 15, 2023|Fixed "Entering Dungeons floor" Autopet rule not working.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Fixed Dungeon Start Pet Rule not working.|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Added "Mythological Mob" and "Sea Creature" options to on kill mob trigger.|
|May 25, 2026|Fixed an exploit with Autopet allowing players to gain the perks of multiple pets at once in certain situations.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Removed [[Wardrobe]] Autopet rule.|
Linked from: [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]], [[Autopet Rules 2-Pack]], [[Changelog]], [[Changelog/2021/February 16]], [[Changelog/2021/September 23]], [[Cold]], [[Flaming Flay]], [[Heat]], [[Soul Whip]]

# Bazaar
Aliases: BZ, Bazzar, NPC Flipping, Special Bazaar
Categories: Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Templates with deprecated argument names
[Bazaar]
The Bazaar is a commodity market for trading items. It is accessed through [[Bazaar (NPC)|Bazaar]] located in the [[Bazaar Alley]], or with the /bazaar [[Commands|command]] if the player has a [[Booster Cookie]] active. [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 7]] is required to access the Bazaar.
## Mechanics
The Bazaar is a commodity market, where players may buy and sell commodity items such as [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]], [[Null Sphere]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] for a price determined by market supply and demand, no one individual has a control over it. Items tradable in the Bazaar cannot be sold on the [[Auction House]].
The Bazaar is split into 5 categories: Farming, Mining, Combat, Woods & Fishes, and Oddities. Clicking on a category will show all the item types or item categories within the category, as well as their buy and sell price. If an item type has multiple products, such as [[Diamond]], clicking it again will show all the different types of products under that item type, and their buy and sell prices. Due to limited number of slots, some items are grouped together under one umbrella category, for example all [[Gemstone]] types can be found under Gemstones and resources originating in [[Dwarven Mines]] under Dwarven Mines.
A toggle is available between Direct and Advanced modes. In Direct mode, only the buy and sell prices of items are shown. In Advanced mode, the number and volume of buy orders and sell offers, as well as the number of instant buys and instant sells within the last 7 days are additionally shown.
### Trading
#### Types of trades
Upon clicking into a specific product, 4 trading options are available: Buy Instantly, Sell Instantly, Create Buy Order and Create Sell Offer.
Placing an Instant Buy request first requires the desired quantity of items. A player cannot instant buy more items than their inventory can hold (71,680 [[Essence]], up to 2,240 for most items, 560 for items such as [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] and 35 for items that have stacks of 1 item,). Confirming the Instant Buy will purchase the items from the current cheapest sell offers available. The Bazaar will quote a price 4% higher than the cheapest sell offer, so that a small change in offers does not restart the buy process. If the order goes through at a price below the additional 4%, the extra [[Coins]] are refunded. If the offers change too much, the purchase will fail and coins will be refunded.
Instantly Selling products takes all items from the inventory and sells them to the best Buy Orders automatically. Right-clicking the Instant Sell button allows the selection of quantity of items to Instantly Sell.
Creating a Buy Order first requires the desired quantity of items. Clicking the sign allows the purchase of up to 71,680 (1,120 stacks) of that item in a single offer, or 256 for unstackable items. Three preset quantities are also available. For most stackable items, they are 64, 160 and 1,024; for most unstackable items, they are 1, 4 and 64.
Upon choosing a quantity, a unit price for the order must be set. Three preset options are available: the same as the current highest buy order, 0.1 [[Coins]] more than the highest buy order, and 5% of the difference between the lowest sell price and highest buy price. A custom price can also be set with the sign, up to 500,000,000 [[Coins]] per item.
After the quantity and unit price are set, the order will be placed. Players who sell products of that type will fill the order.
Creating a Sell Offer is similar. Clicking the Sell Offer button will ask for a price, which can be matching the best offer, 0.1 [[Coins]] below the best offer, 10% of the difference between the lowest sell price and highest buy price, and a custom price. Upon confirming, all items from the inventory (or all of that Essence type, the player has) will be put up on Sell Offer, and players will be able to buy them. By Right-clicking the Create Sell Order button, players can choose a specific amount of how many items to sell.
#### Trading Features
In the Bazaar main menu, the Sell Inventory Now option instantly sells all Bazaar items in their inventory (Essence excluded) and the Sell Sacks Now option will sell all items in their [[Sacks]]. Within the menu of each specific item, players can choose to ignore the given product, doing so will prevent it from being Instantly Sold (sell orders can still be created).
Any active Buy Orders and Sell Offers can be managed with the Manage Orders menu. Here, the active Buy Orders and Sell Offers can be viewed. The number of items and [[Coins]] collected, the % completion and the top vendors of that order can be seen. Items and [[Coins]] can be collected with left click. Right clicking on an order allows it to be canceled, provided no goods or [[Coins]] are left to claim on it. Buy orders which are fully filled can additionally be flipped to sell offer for a set price.
Buy orders and sell offers expire after 7 days, after which the unused [[Coins]] or unsold items can be reclaimed.
A maximum of 14 orders and offers can be placed at once. The [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Bazaar Flipper perk]] (obtainable at the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]]) increases this number by 7 per level up to 14, to a total of 28. In addition, a maximum of 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]] worth of items can be placed on sell offer at once.
There is a 1.25% tax on all Bazaar sales. The [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Bazaar Flipper perk]] decreases this tax by -0.125% per level up to -0.25%, making the tax 1%. The tax is only applied when claiming the order, so different players can sell for varying amounts of [[Coins]] despite selling at the same price. This can also be abused to avoid [[Derpy]]'s 4x taxes.
Clicking on View Graphs in the product view will show a collection of graphs relating to the product. The graph of buy price, sell price, buy order volume, sell offer volume, instant sell volume, instant buy volume, instant sell moving [[Coins]], and instant buy moving [[Coins]] can be seen. The range of these graphs can be changed between 24 hours, 7 days and 31 days by left clicking; the graph can be enlarged with right click. It is not recommended to use the graph as a prediction table as it has a delay of around one day.
#### Overbidding Protection
The Bazaar uses the average price of a product over the previous 7 days to help prevent extreme trades. This average can be viewed by hovering over the product while Advanced Mode is active.
A confirmation popup appears when attempting to purchase an item for more than 2x its 7-day average price. Confirming the purchase prevents the popup from appearing again for 5 minutes.
A similar confirmation popup appears when attempting to Instantly Sell an item for less than 50% of its 7-day average price. Confirming the sale prevents the popup from appearing again for 5 minutes. This warning does not appear when instantly selling multiple items at once through the Sell Inventory Now or Sell Sacks Now options.
Sell Offers cannot be listed for more than 10x the item's 7-day average price.
#### Limit
A maximum of 15,000,000,000 [[Coins]] worth of items can be made in orders on the Bazaar each day, resetting at 12 AM GMT. Orders contribute to this limit as soon as they are made, so they do not need to be filled to increase the limit. The player cannot buy or sell items if it leads to them going over the 15,000,000,000 [[Coins]] limit, although the player can still make orders if the value of the order sums up to less than 1 [[Coins|Coin]]. Orders that are valued at over 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]] will have the excess [[Coins]] not count towards the limit. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

## Special Bazaar
Players on an [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] or [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profile have very limited use of the Bazaar. In these gamemodes, only instantly buying [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] is allowed.
## Trivia
- Players on [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] are completely unable to use the Bazaar.
  - In the first and second [[Bingo]] events, Bingo profiles were granted access to the Special Bazaar, even though Bingo players were not likely to obtain enough coins for a [[Booster Cookie]] in the limited time frame. Starting with the February 2022 event, the Bazaar was made completely unavailable to [[Bingo]] profiles. Instead, players receive 30% more [[Skills|Skill]] experience.
  - Despite being unable to use the Bazaar, it still says you unlocked the Bazaar when you reach [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 7]].
- On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the Bazaar requires [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 12]] instead of [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 7]].
- The prices being determined by player supply and demand is very similar to the effect that Scottish 18th century economist Adam Smith describes as Invisible Hand.

# Block Strength
Aliases: Block Efficiency, Block Hardness, Breaking Strength, Hardness, Instamine, Instant Mining, Tick Thresholds
Categories: Confirmations needed, More info needed, Vanilla objects, Mechanics
[Block Strength]
Category: Mechanics
Block Strength is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Breaking.
Block Strength is a metric based upon a vanilla mechanic that is Mining Efficiency.
## Breaking behaviors on SkyBlock
### Context
- Block Strength does not exist for every instance and for every block, as the mechanics vary from one instance to another. Indeed, using the same tools on the same block in the same circumstances might not yield the same results.
- An historic example of this is Stonking: one could break blocks with limited success in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] but in the [[Dwarven Mines]], the mining progress on the block would be "capped" to a certain point from the perspective of the player, as if they suffered the Mining Fatigue effect, despite using the same tools and targeting the same block type. Since then, [[Dungeonbreaker]] effectively replaced the reliance on this exploit for navigating in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- That said, differences in the breaking behavior can be observed and have been documented in the table below. The finer details of this system are only theorized and it is unknown whether the [[Dungeonbreaker]] relies on Block Strength, [[Toughness]] or something else entirely.

## Mechanics
Block Strength is a metric that determines how fast a block can be broken by the player, tailored for the [[Mining]] skill.
### Formula
- While mining, [[Mining Speed]] is dealt as damage to the block every tick. When enough damage is dealt, the block will break, dropping an item and turning into an unbreakable block for a set period before respawning.
  - If the [[Mining Speed]] is high enough, the block will be mined in 1 tick (or 0.05 second). This is known as the block's Instamining threshold, or 1-tick threshold.
  - Otherwise, damage is limited to 7.5x a block's strength, limiting the break time to 4 ticks (or 0.20 seconds). This is known as a block's softcap.
- The time taken to break a block can be calculated using the following formula:
  - Mining Timein ticks=⌊Block Strength×30Mining Speed⌉
  - Mining Timein seconds=⌊Block Strength×30Mining Speed⌉×120
### Softcap
The softcap limits how fast a block can be mined to at least 4 ticks (or 0.2 seconds). To determine the [[Mining Speed]] needed to reach it for a given block, use the following formula:
- Mining Speed needed to reach Softcap=⌈20×Block Strength3⌉
- The softcap can be bypassed by breaking a block instantly.
### Instant Mining
The softcap can be bypassed if the block is mined within a single Minecraft tick (0.05 seconds). In this case, the block will break instantly.
- To instantly break a [[Blocks|Block]] (except [[Obsidian]]), [[Mining Speed]] needs to be >30x higher than its Block Strength, or >60x if it's a block of [[Obsidian]], an [[Ores|Ore]], a [[Gemstone|Gemstone Block]] or a [[Dwarven Metals|Dwarven Metal Block]].
### Mining Penalty
- A Mining penalty applies x5 on the breaking time.
- A Mining penalty is applied if at least 1 of the following conditions is fulfilled:
  - The player's feet are not on the ground;
  - The player's head is in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] without a Helmet enchanted with [[Aqua Affinity]].
- Unlike in vanilla Minecraft, they do not stack.

## Trivia
### Is 1-ticking [[Gravel]] and [[Mycelium]] truly possible?
- Unlike [[Sand]] and [[Red Sand]], the predicted conditions for 1-ticking [[Gravel]] and [[Mycelium]] based upon the vanilla breaking mechanics are inconsistent with reality.
  - Theory: the calculator predicts 1-ticking [[Gravel]] and [[Mycelium]] should be possible using at least a (A) [[Golden Shovel]] enchanted with [[Efficiency|Efficiency III]] or (B) an un-enchanted [[Golden Shovel]] under the effect of [[Haste Potion|Haste III]]. This supposes that clicking the block instantly breaks it, skipping the block mining animation entirely.
  - Reality: Clicking the block plays the block mining animation for a split second before breaking it. Because Minecraft runs at 20 ticks per second, this implies that ≥2 ticks lapsed since clicking the block. With that said, pressing the Attack Button (without holding it) results in the block being broken.
- Conclusion: with the conditions fulfilled, [[Gravel]] and [[Mycelium]]...
  - can be 1-ticked with a low enough ping;
  - are instamined regardless of ping.
- Discussion:
  - Block toughness difference: has been taken into account when testing all 4 "dig-able" blocks, and does not seem to be the discriminant.
  - Ping: Ping-gliding has been developed to overcome the delay introduced by higher pings, in which the player assumes his time is "one step ahead" of the server's. For example, instead of waiting to witness the block physically breaking, the player will turn his head prematurely and start mining another block expecting the server to stay "a bit behind" and continue chewing through the packets sent by the player earlier. This balance is lost when the player's ping is too inconstant or him being not "gentle enough" with anticipating the server's delay. For example, turning the head around too early/late... or too fast: from the player's perspective, it appears as if some packets were lost.
  - Tampered breaking mechanics: most plausible yet this remains a theory as there is no official documentation to this day.

# Catacombs Mobs
Aliases: Dungeon Mobs, Dungeon mobs, Dungeons Mobs, Mr Dead, Skull (mob)
Categories: Confirmations needed, Mobs, Dungeons, Dungeon Mobs
Catacombs Mobs spawn inside of the [[Catacombs]] [[Dungeons]]. They are enemies who range from weak to strong, and must be killed in order to progress through the dungeon floor. Upon death, they have a chance to drop their respective Dungeon gear.
## Spawning
All Catacombs Mobs spawn only inside Dungeons, whether they spawn naturally or are summoned.
Standard enemies spawn in groups in brown rooms. Some of those enemies will have a yellow star next to their names. When the last mob with a star in the room is killed, it will drop a [[Wither Key]], [[Blood Key]], [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]], [[Revive Stone]] and/or [[Superboom TNT]], as well as marking the room as complete. The more of the Dungeon is explored, the higher the health of the spawned mobs.
## Modifiers
### Damage Resistance
All Catacombs Mobs have [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] resistance: 10% for melee mobs, 30% for ranged mobs, and 50-80% for magic mobs such as [[Withermancer]].
### Room Entering Bonus
When entering a new [[Catacombs Rooms|Room]], all mobs within it will have their [[Health]] and [[Damage]] increased based on the following formula:
NewAmount=CurrentAmount×(1+0.05×(TotalRoomsExploredBefore−1))
The first room entered after starting the run will not have any bonuses, due to no rooms being explored. The amount of squares a room counts for is based on its size. For example, 1x1 rooms take up 1 square, 2x2 rooms take up 4 squares, and L-shaped rooms take up 3 squares. The new room being entered does not count for these squares.
This does not apply to mobs spawned in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] from [[The Watcher]].
### Types
Additionally, each non-[[Bosses|Boss]] mob, also excluding [[The Watcher]]'s summons have a small chance to spawn with a randomly listed modifier below. These show up in the nametag as a prefix, and can also appear and stack with the Runic modifier.
|Prefix|Description|
|-|-|
|Healthy|Grants 1.3x [[Health]], a 5% (1 in 20 kills) chance to drop a [[Healing Tissue]], and a 0.4% (1 in 250) chance to drop a [[Vitamin Death]].|
|Stormy|Grants a 1% (1 in 100 kills) chance to drop Thunderlord VI ([[Thunderlord]]) on death. Every 5 seconds, there is also a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to strike lightning to all players in a 6 block radius. This deals 2,000 [[Damage]] /2,500 [[Damage]] /5,000 [[Damage]] in F5-F7 respectively, and 45,000 [[Damage]] /60,000 [[Damage]] /70,000 [[Damage]] in M5-M7 respectively.|
|Speedy|Grants increased [[Speed]].|
|Fortified|Grants +10% [[Defense]].|
|Flaming|Causes melee attacks to set the player on fire (not negated by the [[Fire Talisman]] or [[Fire Resistance Potion]]).|
### Watcher Modifiers
Mobs spawned by [[The Watcher]] have three possible modifiers.
In [[The Catacombs - Floor II]] and above, the Ooze undead has 1.25x [[Health]], and the Freak undead has 1.2x [[Damage]].
Then, in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] and above, there is a chance for each undead summoned by [[The Watcher]] to have one of the following modifiers. When one is chosen, all undeads summoned spawn with it.
|Modifier|Description|
|-|-|
|Healthy|Grants 1.3x [[Health]], a 5% (1 in 20 kills) chance to drop a [[Healing Tissue]], and a 0.4% (1 in 250) chance to drop a [[Vitamin Death]].|
|Stormy|Grants a 1% (1 in 100 kills) chance to drop Thunderlord VI ([[Thunderlord]]) on death. Every 5 seconds, there is also a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to strike lightning to all players in a 6 block radius. This deals 2,000 [[Damage]] /2,500 [[Damage]] /5,000 [[Damage]] in F5-F7 respectively, and 45,000 [[Damage]] /60,000 [[Damage]] /70,000 [[Damage]] in M5-M7 respectively.|
|Golden|Grants full [[Golden Armor]] and drop 1 [[Gold Essence]] on death.|
|Speedy|Grants increased [[Speed]] and drops Dungeon Potions ([[Dungeon Potion]]) of the equivalent floor ([[Catacombs]]) tier.|
|Stealthy|Makes the mobs invisible, except for their name tags and armor.|
|Boomer|Makes the mobs wear TNT on their head, which is triggered when they die. After a few second it explodes in a 5x5 radius, dealing 100% of the player's [[Health]] as [[True Damage]]. This can be mitigated with [[Absorption]] and the [[Wither Cloak Sword]]. This also drops [[Superboom TNT]].|
At the end, on all floors, each mob has their [[Health]] randomized by an amount between -2,000 and 6,000 [[Health]] based on the following formula:
Max Health=Health+(1000×rand⁡(−2,6))
### Damage Caps
Certain mobs, especially [[Bosses]] and [[Minibosses]], also have a damage cap mechanic similar to that of the [[Magma Cube Boss]]. Any damage it takes above its threshold is reduced by a large amount.

All [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] mobs except [[Angry Archaeologist]] are affected by [[Smite]].

### [[The Watcher]] Undeads
The Watcher will spawn multiple [[Undead (Watcher)|Undead]] mobs with various names and appearances. These include Mr. Dead, Revoker, Tear, Skull, Walker, Cannibal, Frost, Putrid, Reaper, Vader, Flamer, Freak, Leech, Mute, Ooze, Parasite, and Psycho. They can also appear as Wandering Souls.
|Name|Abilities in Floor II+|
|-|-|
|Mr. Dead|Gains Strength for every Undead near it.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Revoker|Can charge towards the player.[Confirm]|
|Tear|Has a Bleeding effect much like the [[Minotaur]].[Confirm]|
|Skull|Can throw a bone like Crypt Lurkers every 12 seconds.[Confirm]|
|Walker|Faster than other Undeads.[Confirm]|
|Cannibal|Targets Berserkers ([[Berserk]])|
|Frost|Targets Mages ([[Mage]])|
|Putrid|Targets Healers ([[Healer]])|
|Reaper|Targets Archers ([[Archer]])|
|Vader|Targets Tanks ([[Tank]])|
|Flamer|Explodes when killed, dealing 1% of nearby players' max [[Health]].|
|Freak|Deals 1.2x [[Damage]].|
|Leech|Heals for 2% of its max [[Health]] every second.|
|Mute|Prevents the player from hearing certain in-game sounds for 5 seconds.|
|Ooze|Has 1.25x [[Health]].|
|Parasite|Spawns 3 Parasites ([[Parasite (Silverfish)]]) when killed.|
|Psycho|Has a 1/3 chance of teleporting behind the player when attacked.|
|Wandering Soul|Wandering Souls take the appearance of players who die in a Dungeon run. When a Wandering Soul is killed, the corresponding player is respawned. Wandering Souls do not spawn if a [[Healer]] is active.|
Additionally, starting from [[The Catacombs - Floor III]], different reincarnations of previous [[Bosses|BOSS]] mobs will start spawning. In [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], a random giant from the [[Sadan]] fight will also spawn.

|[[The Catacombs - Floor V]]|33.3% (1 in 3 spawns)|Bonzo ([[Bonzo (Watcher)]]), Scarf ([[Scarf (Watcher)]]), Spirit Bear ([[Spirit Bear (Watcher)]])|
|[[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]|25% (1 in 4 spawns)|Bonzo ([[Bonzo (Watcher)]]), Scarf ([[Scarf (Watcher)]]), Spirit Bear ([[Spirit Bear (Watcher)]]), Livid ([[Livid (Watcher)]])|

## Trivia
- Skeleton Masters deal a flat amount of [[True Damage]], contrary to popular belief that they deal a percentage of their enemies' health.
- The highest-health normal Dungeon mob (except Deathmite) is the Zombie Lord.
- Bats which die of non-player causes (to fire, lava or other mobs) do not grant their secret.
- Skeletor and Skeletor Prime may be a reference to Terraria, from Skeletron and Skeletron Prime.
- Terracotta, Livid and Bonzo had OptiFine capes for a brief period of time.
  - Terracotta had a cape of the transgender flag. 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] LappySheep purchased the cape.
    - The transgender flag used for the Terracotta was slightly different from the flag used on the [[Yeti]].
  - [[Livid]] had a cape of the flag of India. On February 5, 2021, Reddit user u/TeamSyringe purchased the cape.
  - [[Bonzo]] had a cape of the non-binary flag. Player stepsister___ purchased the cape.
  - On February 7, 2021, the capes were removed with a patch.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Nerfed the [[Damage]] of most Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]]) by 10%, or more for specific mobs.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Nerfed the [[Damage]] and [[Health]] of most non-Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]]) by amounts depending on their floor.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Catacombs Mobs.|
|Jul 10, 2020|Watcher Undeads now have higher Health and lower Defense.|
|Jul 14, 2020|Added Combat XP values to Catacombs Mobs.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Thorn, Spirit Animals, Withermancer, Super Tank Zombie, Super Archer and Frozen Adventurer.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Fixed Lost Adventurers killing themselves after eating their 4th golden apple.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Guardians in The Professor's fight respawn faster.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Unstable version of the Lost Adventurer now deals lightning damage and the Holy version heals.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Added Frozen Adventurers to regular rooms, fixed an issue that was making them too tanky and gave them 1 extra Ice Essence drop.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Fixed Frozen Adventurer dropping the wrong items and Essence numbers.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Thorn now requires 4 hits to kill instead of 5.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Spirit Chicken damage nerfed by approximately 20%.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Fixed Spirit Chickens damaging tanks through their ultimate.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Fixed Super Tank Zombie dropping the wrong items.|
|Jul 31, 2020|Chaos Guardian and Withermancer take reduced ability damage.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Added magic damage resistance to Catacombs Mobs.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Almost every melee enemies now have 10% resistance to magic damage.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Ranged enemies now have around 30% resistance to magic damage.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Magic-type enemies such as Withermancers now have from 50% to 80% magic damage resistance.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Selective bosses and mini-bosses now have some magic damage resistance.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Chaos Guardian magic damage reduction changed from 90% to 80%.|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Added Livid, Livid Enderman Clone, and Fels.|
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Added Sadan, Terracotta, Sleeping Golem, Woke Golem, The Diamond Giant, Jolly Pink Giant, L.A.S.R., Bigfoot, Zombie Commander, Skeletor Prime, and Mimic.|
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Livids are now more likely to teleport to players if they are standing above ground.|
|Oct 1, 2020|0.9.4|Fixed bug with [[Leaping Sword]] which prevented Livid Enderman Clones from spawning.|
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Added Necron, Wither Miner, Wither Guard, Necron's Apostle, Zombie Lord, and Skeleton Lord.|
|Feb 7, 2021|Fixed exploit allowing certain Catacombs Mobs to be given OptiFine capes.|
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Withermancers can now drop [[Conjuring]].|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Added master mode variants for Bonzo, Undead (Bonzo), Scarf, Scarf's minions, The Professor, Thorn, Spirit Bear and Livid.|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Reduced damage of mini-bosses and Skeleton Master.|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Withermancer skulls now only last 10 seconds, instead of persisting indefinitely.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Nerfed the [[Damage]] of most Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]]) by 10%, or more for specific mobs.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Nerfed the [[Damage]] and [[Health]] of most non-Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]]) by amounts depending on their floor.|

# Chocolate Eggs/Chance
Categories: Confirmations needed
Rarity of an obtained [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] depends on the weighted table:
|Rabbit Rarity|Factory Tier; [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] (same as above)|Base Weight|Base Chance|All Buffs Weight|All Buffs Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|608|60.38% (1 in 1.66)|608|51.38% (1 in 1.95)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|250|24.83% (1 in 4.03)|350|29.58% (1 in 3.38)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|2|100|9.93% (1 in 10.07)|150|12.68% (1 in 7.89)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|3|30|2.98% (1 in 33.57)|45|3.80% (1 in 26.3)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|4|10|0.99% (1 in 100.7)|16|1.35% (1 in 73.96)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|5|4|0.40% (1 in 251.75)|6.4|0.54% (1 in 184.91)|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|6|4|0.40% (1 in 251.75)|6.4|0.54% (1 in 184.91)|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|6|1|0.099% (1 in 1,007)|1.6|0.14% (1 in 739.63)|
Additionally, every 250th [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] is guaranteed to be [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and every 1,250th is guaranteed to be [[Rarity|DIVINE]].
Starting at [[Chocolate Factory]] 6, [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit Rabbits]] can be obtained. They have the same weight as a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rabbit. If player obtained every [[Rarity|DIVINE]] [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]], [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rabbit [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]] is replaced with a random [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit]] rabbit. If every Chocobit is already taken, it's replaced with a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rabbit. Progress can be viewed using [[Pity and RNG Meters|/pity]] command.
All rabbits of the same rarity have equal (100) weight. Starting at [[Chocolate Factory]] 5, [[Chocolate Rabbits|Faction Rabbits]] can be obtained. They have half the weight (50) of a normal rabbit. Base weight can be increased with certain methods (additive):
|Source|Weight Increase|Rabbits Affected|
|-|-|-|
|[[Prestige Chocolate Realm]] accessory line|2-10%|Unobtained Rabbits|
|Rabbit Hotspot ([[Chocolate Rabbits]])|50%|Only if on certain island|
Chance of getting a higher rarity [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] can be increased with certain methods (additive):
|Source|Weight Increase|Rabbit Rarity|
|-|-|-|
|Rabbit Shrine|2-40%|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])+|
|Napoleon Rabbit|10%|RARE ([[Rarity]])+|
|Legendary Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|Mythic Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
|Divine Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]) Attribute|1-10%|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|

# Co-op
Aliases: Co-Op, Coop
Categories: Confirmations needed, Mechanics
Co-op (short for cooperation) is a special feature in Hypixel SkyBlock which allows up to 5 players to play the game together on the same island with a co-op [[Profile]] [[Private Island|island]].
Through upgrades from [[Elizabeth]], the number of players allowed in a Co-op profile can be increased (See [[Account & Profile Upgrades]]).
IMPORTANT TIP (SCAM ALERT)
Exercise caution when including people in your co-op profile Due to the unrestrained access rights given by a Co-op profile membership, a player should only be added to a co-op with people that they trust. Since co-op player removal is voting-based, an account added to profile can be used as a voting tool to kick other players. Malicious co-op members could assume ownership of a profile by kicking other players if more than 2 malicious characters are in said profile. Progress lost are not compensated by Hypixel. If a player already in a co-op profile wants to join with a second account, you are advised to reject.
## Chat
The user can send a message to all co-op members in a private chat channel using /coopchat <message> /cchat <message> or /cc <message>. Co-op chat can be toggled by using /chat coop or /togglecoopchat.
## Managing
If the user already has an existing co-op profile, they can use the /coop command, /coopview command or [[Profile|Profile Management]] in their [[SkyBlock Menu]] to manage their co-op profile. If the owner is idle for too long, they may be kicked and items be salvaged.
### Invites
In order for a user to invite someone to a co-op [[Profile]], they should open their [[SkyBlock Menu]] and select Profile Management (nametag item), then right-click on one of the empty profile spot. Alternatively, they could type in the command /coop followed by the username of player(s) they want to invite separated by a space. Note that all the players invited have to be online in SkyBlock. This will create a new profile with a starter [[Private Island]] where the user and their co-op members can build on.
To start a new co-op profile, the player needs to use the command /coop <player 1> <player 2>... and wait for the player(s) to accept.
To add a new member to an existing co-op profile, the user can use the /coopadd <player> command. Using /coopadd on a solo profile will turn it into a co-op profile
To view their current invites, the player can use the /coopview <player> command.
### Kicking
To kick someone from a co-op [[Profile]], the player should use the command /coopkick <player>. This will create a vote with the other co-op members to kick the player. Note that if there are only 2 players in the co-op, you can only kick the other player after 35+ days of inactivity.
### Leaving
The user should make sure they are on a different [[Profile]] before leaving a co-op profile. When leaving a co-op profile, if there's still at least a member on it, the [[Private Island]] will still remain available for them. All the player's items and progress on the co-op profile will be removed and is [[Co-op|salvageable]] by their co-op members.
Warning: Kicking a co-op member, while on a different profile, causes the kick to fail. All members voting in the kick must actively be on the profile while kicking for it to work.
### Salvage
If a co-op member has left/kicked the co-op [[Profile]], the other co-op members can collect their items within 36 hours by using /coopsalvage <player>. However, when salvaging when the person has deleted their profile, it takes time for the game to register this. It should take around 5–15 minutes and the command should work.
## Pros and Cons of joining a co-op
Playing in a co-op has many advantages and disadvantages over playing alone. These are a few:
Advantages::
  Shared Collections: Unlocking a [[Collections|Collection]] recipe also unlocks it for all the other co-op members.
  Minion Experience: Upon gaining [[Skills|Skill]] experience from a [[Minions|Minion]], all co-op members receives a portion of the experience you receive.
  Personal Banks: Playing in a co-op grants access to a bank only you can access. The [[Personal Bank]] has 20% the capacity of the co-op's shared bank and receive 10% interest.
  Auction House: Each additional co-op member adds 3 additional [[Auction House|auction]] slots over the starting max auction slots of 14
Disadvantages::
  Trust: There is a risk of losing progress due to interactions with co-op members that cannot be trusted. There are tactics to kick players out of a co-op.
  Exponential Costs: Playing with more people means having to spend more on everything. this means more money has to be spent on equipment, slayers, dungeon chests, etc
  Shared Auctions: Because the co-op shares [[Auction House|auctions]], this means anyone can set up, cancel, or claim auctions done by any player in your co-op.
## Trivia
- Individual Skill levels are not shared; however, Collections are shared. In Collections menu, members can see how much each members have contributed to a particular collection.
- There was once a glitch in the game allowing players to create co-ops with as many players as they desired, this was soon fixed.
- Co-op members can trade with each other even in [[Ironman]] or [[Stranded]] mode.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Co-op.|
|Jun 17, 2019|Added /cchat as an alias to /coopchat.|
|Sep 10, 2019|Added new Co-op Management menu. (/coop)|
|Sep 10, 2019|Added the ability to kick a co-op member. (/coopkick).|
|Sep 10, 2019|New Historical Members info in the Co-op Management menu to show who has transited through the profiles.|
|Sep 10, 2019|Added the ability to view the status of other Co-op members.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Salvaging a former co-op member's items will now go into the [[Stash]].|
|Nov 2, 2021|Changed the time a Co-op member must be inactive to kick them to 1 week.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Nov 8, 2021|Fixed a bug where salvaging a former co-op member's profile would not salvage their [[Storage]] and Backpacks ([[Backpack]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed being unable to invite ex-members to co-op.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed being unable to invite ex-members to co-op when lifetime members have been maxed out.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Added a confirmation menu to the /coopadd menu that explains the consequences of adding someone to your co-op. All players on the co-op now have to read this menu and press a separate confirm button before voting on if the new player can be added.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Added a new /coopclear command that will clear your pending /coop and /coopadd invites.|

# Cocooning
Aliases: Cacoon, Cocoon, Cocoons
Categories: Confirmations needed, Article stubs
[Cocooning]
Cocooning is a feature that allows killed [[Mob|Mobs]] to respawn.
## Mechanics
Upon triggering a cocoon event, three egg sacs will appear where the mob was killed, shifting for several seconds. After 6 seconds, the killed mob will reappear at full HP [[Health]] . Additionally, the following message will display:
CAUGHT! You cocooned a <mob name>!
The player who cocooned the mob will become its owner, regardless of the original mob's owner.
If the killed mob could spawn as [[Runecrafting|Runic]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] or [[Corrupted Mobs|Corrupted]], the cocooned mob will also have a chance to have those traits too. The cocooned mob acts like a naturally spawned mob, and is eligible for [[Bestiary]], [[Drop Chance|mob drops]], and [[Slayer|Slayer XP]]. The original mob will still be able to naturally respawn while the cocooned mob is alive.
Cocooning still works on mobs spawned from cocooning, allowing players to chain these events.
## Increasing Cocoon Chance
- [[The Primordial]] grants a 10% chance to cocoon [[Slayer|Slayer Bosses]] and [[Slayer|Slayer Minibosses]].
- The [[Reforging|Bloodshot]] reforge grants a 2.5% chance to cocoon most non-boss mobs outside [[Dungeons]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
- The [[Attributes|Cocoon Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants an additional +5% chance to cocoon [[Slayer|Slayer Bosses]] and [[Slayer|Slayer Minibosses]] when using [[The Primordial]].
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
List of mobs that cannot be cocooned:
- Mobs in [[Dungeons]]
- [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Pups]]
- [[Puddle Jumper]]
- [[Zombuddy]]
- [[Timestalk Clone]]
- [[Sphinx]]
- [[Siamese Lynxes|Siamese Lynx]]
- [[Chickzilla]]
- Any Dragon, including [[Ender Dragon]], [[Reindrake]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Withered Dragon]]
- [[Bambuleaf]] and [[Mochibear]]
- [[End Stone Protector]]
- Some fish shard entities, like [[Lotusfish]] or [[Azure]]
- Multi-layered mobs, like [[Scatha]] or [[Fire Eel]] [Confirm]
- [[Wiki Tiki]] and [[Wiki Tiki|Wiki Tiki Laser Totem]]
- [[Jockey Skeleton]]
- Mobs riding other entities, like [[Rider of the Deep]], cannot be cocooned, meaning only bottom part rolls for cocoon chance.
- [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]][Confirm]
- [[Hideonleaf]]
- [[Primordial Bat]], [[Primordial Hatchling]] and [[Primordial Minion]]
- [[Rollim]] and [[Kaus]]
Mobs that can be cocooned despite being bosses include:
- Crimson Isle [[Minibosses]] and related entities, like [[Mage Outlaw|Summoned Skulls]] (intended to be non-cocoonable, but this behavior is currently bugged)
- [[Arachne]]
## Interactions
- If the cocooned mob is a slayer boss, an additional "YOU COCOONED YOUR SLAYER BOSS" appears in chat, and the next slayer quest skips the spawning phase as if the slayer boss has already spawned.
## Gallery
- A cocoon
- A cocoon segment
- An example of a zombie being respawned
## Bugs
- If the cocooned mob is a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]], the cocooned mob will not be considered as a Sea Creature and can be damaged while the player is wearing [[Trophy Hunter Armor (disambiguation)|Trophy Hunter Armor]]. Additionally, the damage buff towards sea creatures ability on the [[Flaming Flay]] and the [[Soul Whip]] will also not work.
- If a [[Hidden Jerry]] is Cocooned, it does not drop any loot upon being killed.
## Trivia
- For mobs such as the [[Fiery Scuttler]] where multiple entities belong to one single [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]], all associated entities are eligible for a cocoon event. The cocoon also will spawn multiple entities instead of the single one that was cocooned.
- If the cocooned mob was a Jawbus Follower summoned by [[Lord Jawbus]], the cocooned mob will return to its original name instead of keeping the Jawbus Follower name.
- The egg sacs bear a resemblance to the sacs that the [[Tarantula Broodfather]] spawns.
- A cocooned [[Phantom Spirit]][Confirm] or [[Timestalk Clone]] (while it could still be cocooned) will randomly take an Alex or Steve skin.
- While it is not normally possible to cocoon mobs in [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] due to no [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] item or other mechanic existing to do so, if the player is somehow able to wear [[The Primordial]] in the Rift, it is able to cocoon the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]].
  - This was verified on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] during the release of the feature with the help of [[Centauri]].
## History

|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Added [[The Primordial]].|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Added [[Shriveled Cornea]].|
|Apr 30, 2026|Added a message when Cocooning a [[Mob]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Added [[The Primordial]].|
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Added [[Shriveled Cornea]].|
|Jul 10, 2025|Disabled Cocooning.|
|Jan 12, 2026|Re-enabled Cocooning.|

|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Cocooned mobs now belong to the player who cocooned them|
